### [1] [Quasi-hostless LRDs with intrinsically red, faint UV continua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28209)
*Callum Witten,Andrea Weibel,Pascal A. Oesch,Rohan P. Naidu,Jorryt Matthee,Alba Covelo-Paz,Romain A. Meyer,Albert Sneppen,Parker Fairfield,Anna de Graaff,Christina C. Williams,Zhiyuan Ji,Pratika Dayal,Yoshinobu Fudamoto,Garth Illingworth,Cristian Carvajal-Bohorquez*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 从132个小红点(LRD)光谱样本中，以FUV与光学连续谱比 L1500/L5100<0.03 为判据，新识别出3个"准无宿主"BH*-like天体，使该类样本扩大一倍以上；其UV谱指数随该比值下降由β_UV≈−1.8红化至≈−0.5，可用中性氢n=2能级高柱密度屏(N_H,n=2≈10^17 cm^-2)加弱宿主模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有BH*(黑洞星)样本的选择依赖BH*模板主导静止光学波段，对静止紫外完全不做约束，可能遗漏或误判那些中心引擎极红、UV极弱的"最纯粹"LRD。作者希望以UV–光学的相对强弱作为独立选择量，检验是否存在一类由中心引擎内在红UV连续谱主导、近乎无宿主的LRD族群。

Method: 在2≲z≲9的132个LRD光谱样本中，选取低FUV–光学连续谱比(L1500/L5100<0.03)的天体，形态上类比MoM-BH*与The Cliff；统计其β_UV随L1500/L5100与L3500/L5100的变化趋势，并检验Balmer decrement与禁线的强弱，最后用"黑体+氢星云发射经中性氢n=2能级高柱密度屏"加弱宿主星系的简单模型拟合红化，并讨论CE替代情景与可检验的UV形态随波长演化。

Result: 新分类出3个BH*-like天体(2个z>6，1个z~3)，使现有样本扩大一倍以上。β_UV随NUV/FUV与光学比下降而系统性变红，从L1500/L5100~0.5处的−1.8升至<0.03处的−0.5，超出该红移处的典型值。随UV/光学比减弱，被气体包裹的BH发射趋于主导，呈现"准无宿主"区制：微弱且红的UV连续谱、极端Balmer decrement、弱禁线。该红化可由黑体+氢星云发射透过N_H,n=2≈10^17 cm^-2中性氢屏的模型配合相对弱的宿主星系再现，替代性中心引擎情景亦可解释。

Conclusion: 存在一族"准无宿主"LRD，其共同特征是来自中心引擎、内禀偏红的静止UV连续谱；UV–光学连续谱比可作为识别BH*主导天体的有效、独立于光学模板的选择量。模型预言宿主星系FUV贡献随波长变化，从而给出可检验的UV形态–波长演化，可用以判别中性氢屏模型与其他中心引擎解释。

Abstract: High-redshift objects characterised by very weak UV relative to their strong, red rest-optical continua, with broad Balmer lines, appear to represent little red dots (LRDs) in their purest form: sources dominated by emission from their central engine (CE), sometimes dubbed black hole stars (BH*s). Previous work has selected BH*-dominated objects, where a BH* template dominates the rest-optical emission; this remains agnostic to the rest-UV. We therefore select low FUV-to-optical continuum ratio ($L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}<0.03$) objects resembling MoM-BH* and The Cliff, from a spectroscopic sample of 132 LRDs at $2\lesssim z\lesssim9$. This identifies three LRDs that we newly classify as BH*-like objects, more than doubling the existing sample: two at $z>6$ and one at $z\sim3$. The UV slope reddens as the NUV- ($L_{\rm 3500}$) and FUV-to-optical ratios fall, with $β_{\rm UV}$ rising from $-1.8$ at $L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}\sim 0.5$ to $-0.5$ at $L_{\rm 1500}/L_{5100}<0.03$, beyond values typically seen at these redshifts. As the UV-to-optical ratio weakens, gas-enshrouded BH emission appears to dominate, leading to the quasi-hostless regime: a weak, red UV continuum emerges alongside extreme Balmer decrements and weak forbidden line emission. This reddening could be reproduced by a simple model of a blackbody and hydrogen nebular emission leaking through a high column density screen of neutral hydrogen in the $n=2$ level ($N_{\rm H,\,n=2}\approx10^{17}\,{\rm cm^{-2}}$), combined with a relatively weak host galaxy, although alternative CE scenarios may also reproduce this reddening. The host galaxy's growing FUV contribution in this model results in a testable UV morphological evolution with wavelength. This work identifies a family of quasi-hostless LRDs that share a common, intrinsically red, rest-UV continuum originating from their central engine.

</details>


### [2] [Resolved internal dynamics of a globular cluster candidate in a cosmological simulation at cosmic dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28212)
*Raffaele Pascale,Francesco Calura,Enrico Vesperini,Alessandro della Croce,Joakim Rosdahl,Alessandro Lupi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用亚秒差距分辨率、可分辨单个恒星粒子的宇宙学模拟，首次直接追踪一个球状星团前身天体的引力塌缩与组装过程及其内部动力学和角动量演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球状星团是宇宙中最古老、最致密的恒星系统，其致密尺度和古老年龄暗示它们必须在早期宇宙快速组装，但具体形成机制仍不确定；已有多种理论设想，然而宇宙学模拟迄今无法分辨其形成细节与诞生时的内部动力学结构。

Method: 采用同时具备亚秒差距空间分辨率和单个恒星粒子形成的宇宙学模拟，追踪一个具有球状星团前身质量与尺度的致密恒星系统的引力塌缩与组装，直接分辨其内部动力学并追踪角动量的积累过程。

Result: 该系统在z≈10.5时于约3 Myr内完成大部分恒星质量组装，迅速变为贫气体、恒星主导，并通过不同恒星子系统的层级并合获得有序的内禀转动；它形成于一个M_vir≈1.73×10^8 M_⊙暗物质晕维里半径内、一个更大恒星复合体的卫星系统。其密度、尺度和质量与JWST在高红移新观测到的致密恒星系统相重叠。

Conclusion: 球状星团前身天体在形成后不久即可具备明确的内部运动学结构，表明早期宇宙中致密恒星系统的快速组装自然伴随有序转动的建立。

Abstract: Globular clusters (GCs) are among the oldest and densest stellar systems in the Universe. Their compact sizes and old ages imply they must have assembled rapidly in the early Universe, yet the detailed physical mechanisms responsible for their formation remain uncertain. Although several scenarios have been proposed, cosmological simulations have so far been unable to resolve their detailed formation and internal dynamical structure at birth. Using a cosmological simulation that uniquely combines sub-parsec resolution with the formation of individual star particles, we follow the gravitational collapse and assembly of a compact stellar system with GC-progenitor mass and size, while directly resolving its internal dynamics and tracing the build-up of its angular momentum. The system assembles most of its stellar mass within 3 Myr, at $z\simeq10.5$, it rapidly becomes gas-poor, star-dominated, and develops ordered internal rotation acquired largely through the hierarchical assembly of distinct stellar subsystems. It forms as a satellite of a larger stellar complex within the virial radius of the $M_{\rm vir}\simeq1.73\times10^8\,M_\odot$ dark-matter halo hosting it. Its density, size and mass overlap with those of compact stellar systems recently observed by JWST at high redshift. These results indicate that GC progenitors can have well-defined internal kinematic structures already after their formation.

</details>


### [3] [Modeling extraordinary relativistic jets on the scale of the cosmic web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28376)
*Gourab Giri,Marco Tavani,Dario Borgogno,Andrea Botteon,Rossella Cassano,Valerio Vittorini,Claudio Gheller,Gianfranco Brunetti,Paul J. Wiita,Tiziana Venturi,Bernie Fanaroff,Andrea Mignone,D J Saikia,Somak Raychaudhury*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用三维相对论MHD模拟，在低密度星系群环境中以3.5×10^46 erg/s的喷流功率成功重现了约7 Mpc的巨型射电星系"Porphyrion"，其喷流在约20 Myr内扩张至约9 Mpc，大部分演化阶段以相对论速度传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的超大质量黑洞喷流尺寸可达宇宙网纤维的典型尺度（如红移0.896处J152932.16+601534.4，即"Porphyrion"，总长~7 Mpc），这对现有相对论喷流MHD模型构成挑战——此前从未成功应用于如此极端的距离，需要苛刻的喷流-环境与流动稳定性条件。作者旨在检验此类巨型喷流能否在现实物理条件下被建模。

Method: 采用最先进的三维相对论磁流体力学（ relativistic-MHD）数值模拟，设定喷流动力学功率为3.5×10^46 erg/s，外部介质为低密度星系群环境；并将该情形与相同功率但在更致密宇宙结构中传播的喷流进行对比分析。

Result: Porphyrion的观测性质可在现实情景下被成功复现；一对喷流在约20 Myr内扩张至约9 Mpc，表明其大部分演化期间以相对论速度增长；对比致密环境下同功率喷流，其外流显著更慢、仅能延伸数Mpc，与已观测到的巨型射电星系主体的统计分布一致。

Conclusion: 低密度宇宙网区域中的高功率喷流可成长为数十Mpc量级的巨型结构，这为观测统计提供了可检验预言：未来巡天与数据库搜寻若发现更大尺度喷流，将有助于研究这一类了解甚少的强功率喷流族群的数量分布与反馈效应。

Abstract: Recent observations of supermassive black hole jets extending up to the typical sizes of cosmic web filaments challenge our current understanding. In particular, the overall jet size of J152932.16+601534.4 ("Porphyrion") reaches $\sim 7$ Mpc at redshift 0.896. Magneto-hydrodynamic (MHD) models of relativistic jets have never been successfully applied to such extraordinary distances, requiring demanding jet-environment and flow stability conditions. By using state-of-the-art numerical simulations of 3D relativistic-MHD jets, we show that Porphyrion's observational properties can be successfully modelled in a realistic scenario, involving a jet kinetic power of $3.5 \times 10^{46}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$ and an external medium of a low-density galaxy group. We find that the pair of jets expands to $\sim 9$ Mpc in about $20$ Myr, implying that the jet grows with relativistic speeds for most of its evolution. Comparing this case with other jets of similar power propagating in substantially denser cosmic structures, we find much slower outflows that can only reach a few Mpc, consistent with the bulk of the observed population of giant radio galaxies. Our results make a testable prediction, i.e. future detections of even larger-scale jets in upcoming surveys and database searches will be invaluable in studying the demographics and feedback of the poorly known population of powerful jets, particularly in under-dense regions of the cosmic web.

</details>


### [4] [The JWST Resolved Stellar Populations Early Release Science Program. X. Cross-Observatory Proper Motion Measurements with HST and JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28381)
*Jack T. Warfield,Nitya Kallivayalil,Sangmo Tony Sohn,Christopher T. Garling,Andrew B. Pace,Roeland P. van der Marel,Daniel R. Weisz,Mark A. Fardal,Niusha Ahvazi,Jay Anderson,Kaia R. Atzberger,Paul Bennet,Martha L. Boyer,Roger E. Cohen,Andrew A. Cole,Matteo Correnti,Andrew E. Dolphin,Marla C. Geha,Mario Gennaro,Kevin A. McKinnon,Kristen B. W. McQuinn,Max J. B. Newman,Ekta Patel,Karin M. Sandstrom,Alessandro Savino,Evan D. Skillman,Eduardo Vitral,Laura L. Watkins,Benjamin F. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 首次结合HST与JWST成像，测得孤立的矮星系WLM的绝对系统自运动（PM），精度达约0.02 mas/yr，并与Gaia结果一致；验证了跨天文台PM测量的可行性与系统误差量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系绝对系统自运动是约束其轨道、质量及暗物质分布的关键观测量，但此前仅有Gaia可在少数近距离星系上提供此类测量。作者希望证明HST与JWST联合成像能扩展这一能力，并系统评估跨天文台测量中的系统误差与滤光片选择问题。

Method: 以约1 Mpc处的孤立矮星系WLM为对象，用背景星系作为参考系：ACS F814W在2015与2024两个历元、NIRCam在2022与2024两个历元成像。分别对每个NIRCam探测器独立拟合，用经验推导的卷积核匹配PSF，并利用注入星系改正偏差；比较不同NIRCam滤光片（F090W、F150W、F200W、F250M、F430M）与HST-To-HST结果的一致性。

Result: 两历元ACS在8.93年间基于145个星系给出(μ_α*, μ_δ)=(+0.087±0.019, −0.079±0.017) mas/yr，是WLM迄今最精确的PM，与Gaia结果在0.4σ内一致。F090W第二历元在7.02年间基于43个星系给出(+0.081±0.045, −0.113±0.046) mas/yr，与同区域HST-To-HST结果在1σ内一致（F150W亦然），而F200W偏差约2σ；长波F250M与F430M在1σ内一致但精度较低。不能排除约0.05 mas/yr、7年基线上的跨天文台系统误差。相同基线与区域下，F090W第二历元精度不低于第二个ACS历元。

Conclusion: HST–JWST联合测量可给出可靠的星系绝对系统自运动，推荐选用与第一历元滤光片波长最接近的NIRCam滤光片；长波滤光片对更近目标或更长基线具有潜力。

Abstract: We present the first absolute systemic proper motion (PM) of a galaxy measured by combining Hubble Space Telescope (HST) and JWST imaging. The PM of WLM, an isolated dwarf galaxy at $\sim$1 Mpc, is measured against background galaxies with Advanced Camera for Surveys (ACS) F814W imaging from 2015 and 2024 and NIRCam imaging from 2022 and 2024. The two ACS epochs give a systemic PM of $(μ_α^*, μ_δ) = (+0.087 \pm 0.019, -0.079 \pm 0.017)$ mas yr$^{-1}$ from 145 galaxies over 8.93 years, which agrees with previous Gaia-based PMs to within 0.4$σ$ and is the most precise PM of WLM. For the HST-to-JWST PMs, we fit each NIRCam detector separately, match the point-spread functions with empirically derived convolution kernels, and correct biases with injected galaxies. With an F090W second epoch, the PM is $(+0.081 \pm 0.045, -0.113 \pm 0.046)$ mas yr$^{-1}$ from 43 galaxies over 7.02 years, which, like the F150W PM, agrees with HST-to-HST over the same area to within 1$σ$, while F200W differs by $\sim$2$σ$. The long-wavelength F250M and F430M PMs are consistent to within 1$σ$, though less precise. Fewer galaxies can be used at wavelengths farther from F814W. A cross-observatory systematic of $\sim$0.05 mas yr$^{-1}$ cannot be ruled out over 7 years. At equal baselines, an F090W second epoch is at least as precise as our second ACS epoch over the same area. We recommend the NIRCam filter closest in wavelength to the first-epoch filter, though the long-wavelength filters show promise for closer targets or longer baselines.

</details>


### [5] [Age dependence of the radial metallicity gradient in the Galactic disc with LAMOST and Gaia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28456)
*Yi-De Jing,Duojie Jia,Bin Wang,Yulong Gao,Liyuan Lu,Zheng-Jun Du,Zenghui Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于LAMOST DR7、Gaia DR3与StarHorse年龄星表的58万颗太阳邻域FGK矮星/转折星样本，研究发现银盘径向金属丰度梯度—年龄关系（MGAR）呈非单调形态：中等年龄梯度最强为负，随后向年老星族显著变平；中老年变平是最稳健特征，而中龄极小值的位置与深度及年轻端形态不稳定，恒星参数选择可影响MGAR形态的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究报告的银盘径向金属丰度梯度随年龄的变化（MGAR）形态存在多样性，其可靠性受样本选择、恒星参数与年龄标度等因素影响。作者希望用大样本、多星表交叉证认，系统检验MGAR的形态并评估其稳健性。

Method: 使用LAMOST DR7中580,037颗FGK型矮星与转折星，结合Gaia DR3天体测量与经校正的StarHorse年龄星表，按2 Gyr年龄分箱拟合径向[Fe/H]梯度；开展多项稳健性检验：低α星族子样、替代引导半径定义、不同径向拟合范围、垂直轨道选择、随机年龄误差传播、替代年龄标度；并控制年龄相关的Teff–log g分布，以及与APOGEE DR17的同星交叉比较。

Result: 基准MGAR非单调：中等年龄梯度较强为负，6–8 Gyr最负但与4–6 Gyr无显著差异，表现为宽泛的转折；年老端显著变平，且在低α子样、替代半径定义/拟合范围/轨道选择、年龄误差传播与替代年龄标度下均可重现。控制Teff–log g分布会显著削弱梯度幅度并改变年轻端行为；同星APOGEE交叉验证显示控制后年龄依赖在外部[Fe/H]标度下仍可重现。

Conclusion: 中龄至年老阶段的梯度变平是观测MGAR最稳健的特征，而中龄极小值的精确位置与深度、以及年轻端细节形态不够稳定；恒星参数选择（如年龄相关的Teff–log g分布）可导致文献中MGAR形态的差异，是解释结果多样性的重要来源。

Abstract: We investigate the stellar radial metallicity ([Fe/H]) gradient--age relation (MGAR) of the Galactic disc using a solar-neighbourhood sample of 580\,037 F-, G-, and K-type (FGK) dwarf and turn-off stars from LAMOST DR7, Gaia Data Release3, and the corrected StarHorse age catalogue. The fiducial MGAR is non-monotonic, with relatively strong negative gradients at intermediate ages followed by substantial flattening towards older populations. The most negative gradient occurs at $6$--$8$ Gyr in the adopted 2-Gyr bins, although the $4$--$6$ Gyr slope is statistically similar, indicating a broad intermediate-age turnover rather than a sharply defined transition. The flattening persists within the low-$α$ population, indicating that chemical-population mixing alone cannot explain the full-sample relation, and is also recovered under alternative guiding-radius definitions, radial fitting ranges, vertical-orbit selections, propagation of the reported random age uncertainties, and an alternative stellar-age scale. In contrast, controlling the age-dependent $T_{\rm eff}$--$\log g$ distribution substantially reduces the absolute gradient amplitudes and modifies the younger-age behaviour. A same-star comparison with APOGEE DR17 further shows that the broad age dependence of the controlled MGAR is recovered using an external [Fe/H] scale. We therefore identify the intermediate-to-old-age flattening as the most robust feature of the observed MGAR, whereas the precise location and depth of the intermediate-age minimum and the detailed younger-age morphology are less stable. These results indicate that stellar-parameter selection can contribute to the diversity of MGAR morphologies reported in previous studies.

</details>


### [6] [Chemical structure of the solar neighbourhood with DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28475)
*Yi-De Jing,Duojie Jia,Yulong Gao,Zheng-jun Du,Zenghui Zou,Liyuan Lu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于DESI DR1与Gaia天体测量，将太阳邻域FGK矮星按[α/Fe]-[Fe/H]分为低α与高α族群，发现二者化学与运动学性质虽有差异但在速度空间显著重叠，表明化学分类不能唯一对应现今运动学成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清银河系盘不同化学族群（低α/高α）的化学、空间与运动学性质差异，并检验化学分类是否与现今运动学成分一一对应，从而理解盘的形成与演化历史。

Method: 联合DESI DR1光谱与Gaia天体测量构建高质量FGK矮星样本；在[α/Fe]-[Fe/H]平面用高斯混合模型（GMM）分离低α与高α族群；分别测量其金属丰度分布、径向与垂向金属丰度梯度、空间分布及运动学性质（速度弥散等）。

Result: 低α族群更富金属、集中于银盘中平面附近、具有负的径向与垂向金属丰度梯度；高α族群更贫金属、垂向金属丰度梯度更陡、径向金属丰度剖面近乎平坦、运动学更"热"；两族群在速度空间存在显著重叠。

Conclusion: 低α与高α族群具有不同的化学富集历史，但化学分类并不唯一映射到现今运动学成分；观测到的化学-动力学结构可由化学富集、径向重分布与长期动力学演化的共同作用解释。

Abstract: We analyse the chemical, spatial, and kinematic properties of stars in the solar neighbourhood using DESI Data Release 1 combined with Gaia astrometry. A high-quality FGK dwarf sample is separated into low-$α$ and high-$α$ populations using Gaussian mixture modelling in the [$α$/Fe]--[Fe/H] plane. The low-$α$ population is more metal rich, dominates near the Galactic mid-plane, and shows negative radial and vertical metallicity gradients. The high-$α$ population is more metal poor, has a steeper vertical metallicity gradient, an approximately flat radial metallicity profile, and dynamically hotter kinematics. Despite these differences, the two chemically defined populations overlap substantially in velocity space. These results indicate distinct chemical enrichment histories for the low-$α$ and high-$α$ populations, while showing that chemical classification does not map uniquely onto present-day kinematic components. The observed chemo-dynamical structure is consistent with the combined effects of chemical enrichment, radial redistribution, and secular dynamical evolution in shaping the Galactic disc.

</details>


### [7] [Identifying Distant and Highly Reddened Star Clusters: 486 Open Clusters and Candidates in the Dusty Galactic Disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29181)

  <summary>Details</summary>

An exact turning-point integral gives the pericenter advance at finite eccentricity, and an exterior expansion identifies the leading halo contributions. A fifteen-parameter Bayesian analysis combines astrometric and spectroscopic observations with the measured precession parameter, from which we obtain the marginalized $1σ$ upper limits $r_s/M<0.236$, $0.168$, and $0.030$, and $ρ_sM^2<0.536$, $0.578$, and $0.004$, respectively. The leading halo monopole in the first two metrics is degenerate with the central mass. At fixed dynamical mass, their leading radial profile correction produces retrograde precession. The $γ=5/2$ metric introduces an additional angular deficit contribution, which enhances the precession response and accounts for its smaller parameter limits.

Abstract: The Auriga-California Molecular Cloud (AMC) is a nearby star-forming region with a moderate level of star formation activity. Previous infrared surveys have shown that its young stellar population is concentrated along a dense filament extending north of NGC 1579. We searched for new manifestations of ongoing star formation in the AMC by identifying optical and molecular outflows, Hα emission-line stars, and young stars associated with reflection nebulae. Narrowband Hα and [SII] imaging, together with slitless spectroscopy, were obtained with the 1-m Schmidt telescope of the Byurakan Observatory. The optical data were complemented by near-infrared images from the Spitzer Enhanced Imaging Products (SEIP) and by WISE/unWISE archival data. We discovered seven new Herbig-Haro (HH) objects and outflows associated with several young stellar objects, including deeply embedded infrared sources and stars surrounded by compact reflection nebulae. Nearly all of the optical HH objects have molecular hydrogen counterparts, and several additional candidate H2 outflows without optical counterparts were identified. Particularly noteworthy is a compact region around V347 Per that appears to host at least three molecular outflows. We also present a list of newly identified Hα emission-line stars and a catalogue of stars associated with optical reflection nebulae. Both populations are concentrated primarily along the northern dark filament of the cloud. The spatial distribution of the newly identified HH objects, molecular outflows, emission-line stars, and nebulous young stars confirms that the northern filament is the principal site of ongoing star formation in the AMC. The close correspondence between the optical and molecular outflowsfurther suggests that most of their driving sources are deeply embedded Class 0/I young stellar objects.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对多个银河系疏散星团进行统一、自洽的多参数刻画（结构、距离、年龄、质量函数与轨道），以检验其是否在稳定的银盘轨道上演化，并探索其诞生半径与径向迁移历史。

Method: 基于Gaia DR3，用天测与测光判据筛选成员星；以King型面密度模型拟合径向密度剖面得结构参数；用平均视差与自行给出天测距离；采用PARSEC演化模型在MCMC框架下进行贝叶斯等龄线拟合估计年龄等基本参数；借助基于等龄线的质光关系将光度函数转换为现质量函数并拟合斜率；结合Gaia DR3与补充光谱巡天的平均系统视向速度得到LSR系下的银河系空间速度(U,V,W)；用galpy与MWPotential2014势场进行3 Gyr反向轨道积分。

Conclusion: 四个星团在运动学上均符合薄盘特征，整体在相对稳定的银盘轨道内演化；部分星团可能经历了有限的径向迁移，但未见显著的垂向或离心激发。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive photometric, astrometric, and dynamical study of four Galactic open clusters, NGC 1662, NGC 1778, NGC 2422, and NGC 2437, using Gaia DR3 data. Probable cluster members were identified through astrometric and photometric criteria. Radial density profiles were modelled with a King-type surface-density distribution to derive structural parameters. Mean parallaxes and proper motions yielded astrometric distances of 410 +/- 12 pc (NGC 1662), 1761 +/- 335 pc (NGC 1778), 477 +/- 18 pc (NGC 2422), and 1681 +/- 172 pc (NGC 2437). Fundamental astrophysical parameters were estimated through Bayesian isochrone fitting using PARSEC evolutionary models within an MCMC framework. The resulting ages (log(t/yr)) are 8.91 (-0.33/+0.24), 8.20 (-0.11/+0.18), 7.80 (-0.11/+0.06), and 8.56 (-0.34/+0.27), respectively. Luminosity functions were transformed into present-day mass functions using an isochrone-based mass-luminosity relation. The derived mass-function slopes are broadly consistent, within uncertainties, with the classical Salpeter form. Mean systemic radial velocities from Gaia DR3 and complementary spectroscopic surveys enabled the determination of Galactic space velocities (U, V, W) in the Local Standard of Rest frame. Backward orbital integrations over 3 Gyr were performed using galpy with the MWPotential2014 Galactic potential. The resulting orbits are nearly circular (e <= 0.106), with limited vertical excursions (Z_max < 0.3 kpc), consistent with thin-disc kinematics. Inferred birth radii suggest possible outward radial displacement for NGC 2422, NGC 2437, and NGC 1778, and inward displacement for NGC 1662. Overall, all four clusters appear to have evolved within relatively stable Galactic disc orbits.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 两个z~6无透镜HyLIRGs具有极端致密、暖/光学厚尘埃、超爱丁顿星形成面密度与极端[CII]亏损等特征，并具备极短气体耗尽时标，因而是z~5最早大质量宁静星系的可信祖先候选，为研究宇宙黎明大质量星系快速形成与淬灭提供了关键样本。

Abstract: Hyperluminous infrared galaxies (HyLIRGs) at cosmic dawn are key to understanding the formation of the first massive galaxies. Using ALMA and NOEMA observations, we robustly detected CO and [CII] lines in both galaxies and unambiguously confirmed their redshifts to be $z=6.092$ and $z=5.90$, respectively. Panchromatic SED fitting reveals dust-obscured star formation rates of $\approx1000$ and $\approx2300\,M_\odot\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, ranking them among the most intensely star-forming systems known at $z \gtrsim 6$. High-resolution ALMA observations reveal an extremely compact morphology for S2COS-84, with a dust-continuum effective radius of $\approx0.3$ kpc, implying a star-formation rate surface density above the Eddington limit. The far-infrared SEDs of both galaxies, together with the compact size of S2COS-84, indicate warm dust ($T_{\rm dust} \approx 80$ K) that is likely optically thick at far-infrared wavelengths. Both galaxies exhibit an extreme [CII] deficit, with $L_{\rm [C\,II]}/L_{\rm IR} \approx 10^{-3.7}$, an order of magnitude below that of normal star-forming galaxies. Their molecular gas masses, derived consistently from both [C\,{\sc ii}] and dust, together with their IR-derived SFRs imply short gas depletion times of $\approx$40--60 Myr, making them plausible progenitors of the earliest massive quiescent galaxies found at $z\sim5$.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST"小红点"(LRDs)的积分宽Hα线宽在固定线光度下异常集中，但以往宽度测量多基于单一窗口/线心，尚不清楚纳入外翼后这种紧致性是否依然成立；同时质量与运动学模型要求测量口径与模型假设相匹配。

Conclusion: LRDs宽Hα宽度的表观紧致性取决于σ(V)所包含的速度区间，随外翼纳入而退化；因此黑洞质量与核区运动学模型必须同时匹配谱线跃迁(Hα/Hβ)与速度积分区间，方能进行可靠比较。

Abstract: Broad Balmer emission lines probe black hole masses and nuclear kinematics in high-redshift active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Holding each object and its broad-H$α$ luminosity fixed, we measure the finite-window line dispersion $σ(V)$, the flux-weighted second-moment width within $\pm V$, from $V = 1500$ to $4500~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ in JWST/NIRSpec spectra and Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) Type-1 AGNs. Across 20 high signal-to-noise LRDs, the observed scatter in $\log_{10}σ(V)$ at fixed $L_{\mathrm{H}α}$ rises from 0.036 to 0.084 dex between $V = 2500$ and $4500~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, a paired increase of 0.048 dex with a 95% bootstrap interval of 0.026-0.064 dex, compared with 0.020 dex across 167 broader SDSS AGNs. Alongside integrated widths in JWST, SDSS, and 334 Euclid DR1 AGNs, these results show that the observed tightness of the H$α$ scaling depends on the velocities included in $σ(V)$, requiring mass and kinematic models to match both transition and velocity interval.

*Ryan Eskenasy,Yuanyuan Su,Qinnan Zhu,Charles Romero,Junjie Mao,Yuan Li,Tirso Marin-Gilabert,Romana Grossová,Valeria Olivares,Ming Sun,Elke Roediger,Ralph P. Kraft,Sheng-Chieh Lin,Aaron Burke*

Abstract: The oxygen abundance of H II regions is a fundamental tracer of chemical enrichment and gas cycling and is now routinely measured across a broad range of cosmic time. A persistent complication is the abundance discrepancy factor (ADF): the O$^{++}$/H$^+$ abundance inferred from optical recombination lines (RLs) is systematically higher than that inferred from collisionally excited lines (CELs). Small-scale temperature fluctuations within the O$^{++}$-emitting gas are among the most widely discussed explanations for this discrepancy. However, the recent identification of a substantial continuum-pumping contribution to the O II spectrum of M42 motivates a renewed assessment of which observations uniquely favor a temperature-based interpretation and which are also compatible with other mechanisms. This result does not exclude temperature fluctuations in H II regions. Motivation: OH是分子气体（尤其是H2）的重要示踪剂，但其在弥散分子气体中的分布、覆盖因子及与HI/CO的关系尚缺乏大样本、无偏的统计约束。作者希望借助大视场、高灵敏度的MeerKAT吸收线巡天，独立且更精确地测量银河系OH吸收气体的占据情况。

Result: 确认15个OH 1667/1665 MHz吸收探测，多数伴随视觉消光(A_V)过量、CO发射及高比例的HI吸收；另有9个OH 1612 MHz卫星线候选，其中2个与主谱线重合且积分光深比远高于LTE预期的1:9。N(OH)与N(HI)无相关性。考虑灵敏度差异后，log N(OH)>12的OH气体覆盖因子为C=0.051±0.008，并有随银纬降低的定性证据；按A_V过量加权的叠加显示清晰OH吸收信号。物理建模给出氢数密度10–100 cm^-3、典型log N(H2)>21、UV通量与数密度比0.01–0.3 Mathis cm^3，符合冷中性介质(CNM)特征。

Conclusion: OH 18-cm吸收主要示踪含H2的弥散分子气体，其覆盖因子得到独立且更精确的测量，并与A_V、CO及HI吸收一致；结果支持OH作为弥散分子气体与CNM的有效示踪剂。

Abstract: We present a blind search for Galactic OH 18-cm absorption lines towards 19,130 radio sources brighter than 1mJy at 1.4GHz, using 390 pointings of the MeerKAT Absorption Line Survey (MALS). We apply an automated line search followed by Bayesian nested sampling to identify OH 1667MHz, 1665MHz and 1612MHz absorption features, using HI emission and absorption profiles as physically motivated priors. We identify 15 confident detections of OH 1667/1665 MHz absorption. The majority of detections are associated with a visual extinction ($A_{\rm V}$) excess and, within the same MALS pointings, with detected CO emission and a high incidence rate of HI absorption, consistent with OH tracing the H$_2$-bearing medium. We also identify 9 candidates of OH 1612MHz satellite line absorption, two of which coincide with OH main line detections and show integrated optical depth ratios well above the LTE expectation of 1:9. As in previous studies, $N(\rm OH)$ shows no dependence on $N$(HI). Accounting for the varying sensitivity across sight lines, we estimate the covering fraction of OH-bearing gas at $\log N(\rm OH) > 12$ to be $C = 0.051 \pm 0.008$, providing an independent and a more precise estimate than previous measurements of the diffuse molecular gas. We also find qualitative evidence for a decrease of the covering fraction with Galactic latitude. Stacking reveals a clear OH absorption signal that becomes increasingly prominent when sight lines are weighted by $A_{\rm V}$ excess, independently confirming the association of OH with diffuse molecular gas. Modeling of the physical conditions yields hydrogen number densities of 10-100 cm$^{-3}$, typical H$_2$ column densities $\log N(\rm H_2) > 21$, and UV-flux to number density ratios of 0.01-0.3 Mathis cm$^{3}$, typical of the CNM.

Conclusion: AMORE6确为极端贫金属系统，其窄Lyman-alpha线与小动力学质量暗示可能是在原子冷却晕中形成大质量恒星，或为一个引力束缚的星团；Hbeta光度过高可能源于偏离标准复合条件或星族模型局限，需进一步检验。

Abstract: We present deep X-shooter follow-up spectroscopy of AMORE6, an exceptionally metal-poor galaxy at z = 5.725. The X-shooter spectrum also reveals a narrow Lyman-alpha line with a velocity dispersion sigma_v = 23.2 +/- 0.5 km/s and a nearly symmetric profile centered at the systemic redshift measured from Hbeta. Possible explanations include departures from standard recombination conditions or limitations in the adopted stellar population models. Conclusion: flow matching 的摊销式联合后验推断有望实际扩展到完整的 LISA 全局拟合问题，为海量重叠引力波源的群体推断提供可行路径。

Abstract: Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe millions of galactic binaries (GBs) at once, thousands of them resolvable, with signals that overlap in frequency. Separating them requires a global fit, a joint inference of all sources, which today takes Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samplers hours to days on a cluster and is repeated for each new stretch of data. We adapt recent advances in large-scale diffusion models, flow matching with a multimodal transformer, to learn the joint posterior once and then draw from it in a fraction of a second. We evaluate our approach on a realistic simulator, with full instrument response and noise, priors fitted to a simulated Galactic catalogue, and sources sampled anywhere in the entire 0.1 to 16 mHz frequency band. We demonstrate that the approach is scalable to up to 15 653 sources fitted simultaneously, evaluating calibration and coverage w.r.t. the MCMC baseline. The results show that this approach could be realistically scaled to tackle the full global fit problem.

We are searching for the universal configuration that arises if halo magnetization originates from a central Cosmic Battery, and is subsequently transported outward by large-scale outflows. Our analysis considers 10 edge-on galaxies in the field of view of the POSSUM survey with angular sizes between 60 and 100. For each such galaxy we collect all available background RM sources in a circular region with a radius of 2? around it. We rescale all galaxy sizes and their associated background RM source distances to a common galaxy size of 100 and stack all these regions together. We divide the stacked configuration of RM sources into the four quadrants on either side of the stacked galaxy axis. The mean RM of background sources is computed in each quadrant, and we obtain a pattern of positive and negative mean RM values around the stacked galaxy. A consistent, systematic RM-gradient pattern emerges inside about 6 stacked galactic radii, as expected in Magnetic Disk-Winds with the axial magnetic field configuration predicted by the Cosmic Battery. The inferred large-scale axial electric current in the halo flows from the center outwards. Beyond 6 galactic radii we observe the reverse mean RM pattern, as expected in the axial Magnetic Black-Hole Jet configuration predicted by the Cosmic Battery. Our work confirms the prediction of the Cosmic Battery, and presents one of the very few known signatures of the magnetic field in the halo and the circumgalactic medium (CGM).

TL;DR: 构建红移与金属度依赖的IMF（顶端偏重）SED模板，结合半解析星系模型研究其对高红移UV光度函数的影响，发现自洽模型能较好再现z~10观测，但z≳12仍有偏差，且偏好较弱的有效尘埃衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST发现z≳10处亮星系丰度高于预期，有假说认为早期宇宙存在顶端偏重的恒星初始质量函数（IMF），需评估该假说在自洽星系形成框架下的可行性与可观测后果。

Method: 基于Chon et al. (2022)星团模拟与Steinhardt et al. (2020)红移依赖IMF模型，构建随红移和金属度变化的IMF SED模板；将其嵌入基于大盒N体模拟的半解析星系模型，并与Kroupa IMF对比，分别在含/不含尘埃衰减情形下计算紫外光度函数（UVLF）；在z=7处标定尘埃，并对z=9.7与z=13.04的观测UVLF拟合有效尘埃光学深度。

Result: z-Z依赖IMF提升年轻星族（尤其低金属度）的紫外光度，并增大UV光度-SFR转换系数的离散；无尘埃时两种顶端偏重模型均显著抬高UVLF亮端；含尘埃（z=7标定）后，IMF依赖的金属富集与尘埃衰减部分抵消本征增亮，自洽模型较好再现z~10观测，z≳12仍有不符；最佳拟合尘埃光学深度分别为基准值的51%（z=9.7）与21%（z=13.04），最高红移比较无稳健约束。

Conclusion: 评估顶端偏重IMF对高红移星系的影响必须同时计入增强的恒星紫外辐射、IMF依赖的金属富集与尘埃衰减；自洽模型支持较弱的有效尘埃衰减，但仅靠顶端偏重IMF难以完全解释z≳12的观测。

Abstract: JWST observations have revealed a higher-than-expected abundance of bright galaxies at $z \gtrsim 10$. Several scenarios have been proposed to explain this excess, one of which invokes a top-heavy stellar initial mass function (IMF) in the early Universe. In this work, we construct spectral energy distribution (SED) templates based on a redshift- and metallicity-dependent IMF ($z$-$Z$ dependent IMF) motivated by the star-cluster simulations of Chon et al. (2022) and the redshift-dependent IMF model of Steinhardt et al. (2020). We implement these templates in a semi-analytic galaxy model built on a large-box $N$-body simulation to investigate the impact of a top-heavy IMF on the ultraviolet luminosity function (UVLF) of high-redshift galaxies. The adopted $z$-$Z$ dependent IMF enhances the UV luminosity of young stellar populations, particularly at low metallicity, and increases the scatter in the UV luminosity-to-star formation rate conversion factor relative to the Kroupa IMF. Without dust attenuation, both top-heavy IMF models predict a substantially enhanced bright end of the UVLF. However, when dust attenuation is included and calibrated at $z=7$, IMF-dependent metal enrichment and the resulting dust attenuation partly offset the intrinsic UV enhancement. The self-consistent model broadly reproduces the observed UVLF at $z\sim10$, although discrepancies remain at $z\gtrsim12$. For the selected high-redshift UVLF constraints, the best-fitting dust optical depths are 51% and 21% of the fiducial values at $z=9.7$ and $13.04$, respectively, indicating a preference for weaker effective dust attenuation. The highest-redshift comparison does not provide a robust constraint. Our results highlight the importance of accounting for both enhanced stellar UV emission and IMF-dependent metal enrichment and dust attenuation when assessing the impact of a top-heavy IMF on high-redshift galaxies.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文对Euclid DR1强引力透镜搜寻（ECLIPSE星表，>15000个候选体，1900 deg²）中使用的五个神经网络进行比较分析，揭示不同网络对图像信息的利用差异及其选择偏置，并评估融合策略对星表完备性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECLIPSE星表由五个独立开发的网络生成，这些网络的图像处理与训练选择各不相同，因而找到的系统也不同。作者希望理解这些差异的来源，以及它们如何影响星表完备性与候选体多样性，为后续数据发布及LSST、Roman等巡天项目的网络组合策略提供依据。

Method: 在50 deg²标定集及完整DR1样本上评估五个网络（含卷积神经网络与视觉Transformer，使用相同数据池但图像处理与训练选择不同），比较其打分行为，并在2000个目标的目视检查预算下考察网络组合效果；同时分析网络在爱因斯坦半径、光变轮廓、质量分布几何、测光红移与星等维度的偏好。

Result: 各网络对候选体的打分差异显著；在标定集上以2000个检查预算组合网络可回收500余个透镜候选体。在完整DR1样本上，单个网络表现出明显偏置：部分网络偏好大爱因斯坦半径系统，另一些对紧凑构型更敏感；还观察到网络在透镜光轮廓、质量分布几何、测光红移与星等上的偏好差异。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了神经网络选择偏置如何塑造Euclid DR1 ECLIPSE星表，为未来Euclid数据发布及LSST、Roman等宽场巡天中网络的组合方式提供指导，以兼顾透镜样本的完备性与多样性。

Abstract: The Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1) strong lens search presents ECLIPSE, the largest catalogue of strong gravitational lens candidates to date, with >15 000 systems identified across 1900 deg2 of sky. It was constructed using five neural networks applied to approximately 68 million galaxies, followed by citizen science and expert visual inspection. In this work, we compare how the independently developed networks use image information to identify their selection of strong lens candidates. The search combines convolutional neural networks and a vision transformer, using the same pool of data but with different image processing and training choices. We investigate why these networks find different systems and what that means for the completeness of our catalogue. We evaluate all networks on a 50 deg2 calibration set and their application to the full DR1 sample. We find that the networks score candidates very differently, and that combining them recovers over 500 lens candidates within our calibration set at an inspection budget of 2000 objects. On the full DR1 sample, we show that individual networks have distinct biases: some networks favour systems with a larger Einstein radius while others are more sensitive to compact configurations. We also investigate network preferences in terms of lens light profile, mass distribution geometry, photometric redshift, and magnitudes. These findings provide a detailed analysis of how neural network selection biases have shaped the Euclid DR1 ECLIPSE catalogue. The results inform how networks should be combined in future Euclid data releases and upcoming wide-field surveys such as LSST or Roman to maximise both completeness and diversity of the recovered lens population.

</details>

TL;DR: ACT DR6联合Planck、CMB透镜与DESI BAO数据偏好更高的标量谱指数与轻微正的跑动，使Starobinsky模型陷入张力。作者通过放松标量扰动的Bunch-Davies初态假设（引入激发参数χ_S与相对相位2φ_S），在不修改暴胀势的前提下恢复了与数据的符合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACT DR6等新数据给出偏高的n_s与轻微正的α_s，使Starobinsky模型受张力、二次混沌模型因r过大被排除。作者希望在不改动暴胀势的前提下，仅通过改变原初扰动的初始量子态来缓解这一矛盾。

Method: 用Bogoliubov系数参数化激发初态，引入激发参数χ_S和相对相位2φ_S，推导其标度依赖对n_s、α_s的修正量；在(φ_S,χ_S)平面上映射满足ACT DR6的1σ区间、r<0.038以及新物理能标Λ>H的区域，并对Starobinsky与二次混沌两模型分别给出对dχ_S/dlnk、d²χ_S/d(lnk)²的解析约束。

Result: Starobinsky模型：标度依赖的χ_S或φ_S几乎在整个平面可行，仅φ_S=0,π附近且χ_S≳1.22的小邻域被排除；标度依赖相位在φ_S=0,π/2,π及χ_S=0处发散。QC模型需χ_S≳0.63且Λ≲6H以压低r，并要求0.0021≲dχ_S/dlnk≲0.0058、0.0008≲d²χ_S/d(lnk)²≲0.0062；两模型均需|dχ_S/dlnk|≳0.0021。

Conclusion: 激励初态可在不改势的前提下使Starobinsky与QC模型重新符合ACT DR6数据；若张量模保持BD态，则预言一致性关系r=-8n_T被破坏，可作为未来张量谱及其倾斜测量的检验。

Abstract: Data Release 6 of the Atacama Cosmology Telescope, combined with Planck, CMB lensing and DESI BAO data, favours a higher scalar spectral index $n_{s}$ than Planck and a mildly positive running $α_{s}$, putting the Starobinsky model in tension with the data, while the quadratic chaotic model is already excluded by its large tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$. We show that relaxing the Bunch--Davies assumption for the initial state of the scalar perturbations can restore agreement without modifying the inflaton potential. Parametrising the Bogoliubov coefficients by an excitation parameter $χ_{S}$ and a relative phase $2\varphi_{S}$, we derive the shifts of $n_{s}$ and $α_{s}$ induced by their scale dependence and map the regions of the $(\varphi_{S},χ_{S})$ plane compatible with the $1σ$ ACT DR6 ranges, the bound $r<0.038$, and the requirement that the scale of new physics $Λ$ exceed the Hubble scale $H$. For the Starobinsky model, a scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ or $\varphi_{S}$ works almost everywhere in this plane: only small neighbourhoods of $\varphi_{S}=0,π$ with $χ_{S}\gtrsim1.22$ are excluded, and a scale-dependent phase fails at $\varphi_{S}=0,π/2,π$ and at $χ_{S}=0$, where the required ${\rm d}\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k$ and ${\rm d}^2\varphi_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2$ diverge. For the QC model, the excited state must also suppress $r$, which requires $χ_{S}\gtrsim0.63$ and $Λ\lesssim6H$. A scale-dependent $χ_{S}$ must satisfy $|{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k|\gtrsim0.0021$ in both models, and in the QC model we show analytically that $0.0021\lesssim{\rm d}χ_{S}/{\rm d}\ln k\lesssim0.0058$ and $0.0008\lesssim{\rm d}^2χ_{S}/{\rm d}(\ln k)^2\lesssim0.0062$. If the tensor modes remain in the BD state, the scenario also predicts a violation of the consistency relation $r=-8n_{T}$, testable once the tensor power spectrum and its tilt are measured.

</details>


### [29] [On the distribution function for cross-correlation estimators in cosmology and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28218)
*Robert N. Cahn,Marc Kamionkowski*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出高斯随机变量交叉相关估计量的分布（χ²分布的交叉相关类比）的两种教学式推导，并讨论其性质与宇宙学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方差估计量服从χ²分布已被广泛使用，但两独立高斯变量交叉相关估计量的分布在同一领域中尚未被普遍掌握，而宇宙学交叉相关测量等场景需要该分布来解释数据。

Method: 针对两个相互独立的标准高斯变量x、y，考察估计量N^{-1}Σx_i y_i（期望为零、方差为一），给出该分布的两条教学式推导，并分析其若干性质。

Result: 得到了交叉相关估计量的显式分布函数（χ²分布的交叉相关对应形式），并通过两种推导相互印证其正确性。

Conclusion: 该分布虽已见于文献但未被广泛内化；本文将其系统化呈现，重点讨论与宇宙学观测量（如交叉相关测量）的联系，并指出该结果在物理学其他领域的适用性。

Abstract: The ubiquitous Gaussian distribution, $e^{-x^2/2}/\sqrt{2π}$, has mean of zero and variance of 1. It is well known that the estimator $\widehat{x^2} = N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^N x_i^2$ for the variance obtained from $N$ samples $x_i$ is drawn from a $χ_n^2$ distribution, a result used often to assess the implications of data for a model. One often also needs to measure the covariance, or cross-correlation, of two different variables $x$ and $y$. If the distributions for $x$ and $y$ are both gaussian but independent of each other, the estimator $N^{-1}\sum_i x_i y_i$ for the cross-correlation is distributed about its expectation value (zero) with variance unity. The distribution function for this estimator may also be required to interpret data, for example in the measurement of cosmological cross-correlations. This distribution---the cross-correlation analog of the $χ_n^2$ distribution---appears in the literature, but it has not been widely internalized by many in the field. We therefore here present two pedagogical derivations of this distribution and discuss some of its properties. We discuss in particular the connection to cosmological observables, but note several other areas of physics where these results may apply.

</details>


### [30] [Scalar dynamics in a subclass Ia mimetic DHOST bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28463)
*Idaiane L. Machado,Yi-Fu Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在二次型Ia类DHOST实现的无奇点反弹宇宙中，约化后的标量模无空间梯度项，物理声速为零（c_s²=0），有效频率平方与波数无关且从下方趋于零，导致原mimetic模型中用于设定正频初始条件的渐近振荡区消失，Wronskian条件无法唯一确定初始态，因此渐近动力学本身不能唯一确定原初标量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前在含高阶导数的mimetic引力框架下分析过一个无奇点反弹宇宙，并利用远收缩阶段的渐近振荡区施加正频初始条件来确定原初标量谱。本文旨在检验该模型对应的二次型Ia类DHOST实现中，这一初始条件设定机制是否依然成立，即标量扰动动力学与谱的可预言性是否依赖于框架选择。

Method: 对标量扇区做ADM分解与Hamilton约化，并施加mimetic约束与动量约束；对Ia类DHOST子类的标量势做常数重标度以匹配原模型的标度因子历史（拉格朗日乘子一般不同）；聚焦原模型声速为正的分支0<γ<2/3，比较约化后的正则标量模结构及匹配反弹背景上的有效频率平方。

Result: 约化后的正则标量模不含空间梯度贡献，物理声速c_s²=0，与α_L=0的mimetic-DHOST扇区已知的真空行为一致；在匹配的反弹背景上，有效频率平方不再依赖共动波数，并在远收缩阶段从下方趋近于零，因而原模型中用于施加正频初始条件的渐近振荡行为不存在。

Conclusion: 尽管仍可通过Wronskian条件实现正则归一化，但它不能唯一选定初始态；在没有额外态指定规则的情况下，渐近动力学无法唯一确定原初标量谱。这表明该类反弹模型的标量谱预言对DHOST实现方式与初始态选取敏感。

Abstract: We study scalar perturbations in a quadratic subclass Ia degenerate higher-order scalar-tensor (DHOST) realization of a nonsingular bouncing cosmology previously analyzed in higher-derivative mimetic gravity. After a constant rescaling of the scalar potential, the subclass Ia model reproduces the same scale-factor history as the original model, although the associated mimetic Lagrange multiplier generally differs. For the comparison with the original mimetic model, we focus on the branch $0<γ<2/3$, for which the scalar sound speed of the original model is positive. We perform an ADM and Hamiltonian reduction of the scalar sector and show that, imposing the mimetic and momentum constraints, the reduced canonical scalar mode contains no spatial-gradient contribution. The physical scalar sound speed therefore vanishes, $c_s^2=0$, in agreement with the known vacuum behavior of the \(α_L=0\) mimetic-DHOST sector. On the matched bouncing background, the resulting effective frequency squared is independent of the comoving wavenumber and approaches zero from below in the remote contracting phase. Consequently, the asymptotic oscillatory regime used in the previous mimetic model to impose positive-frequency initial conditions is absent. Although canonical normalization can still be imposed through the Wronskian condition, it does not select the initial state uniquely. Without an additional state prescription, the asymptotic dynamics therefore does not uniquely determine the primordial scalar spectrum.

</details>


### [31] [Wavelet-CFM for the extragalactic millimeter-wave sky](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28695)

Abstract: We develop a Wavelet-factorized Conditional Flow Matching (Wavelet-CFM) framework to generate fast, accurate simulations of correlated astrophysical fields including gravitational lensing convergence ($κ$), the kinetic and thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ and tSZ) effects, and cosmic infrared background (CIB) emission. Together these make up every bright extragalactic component of the millimeter-wave (mm-wave) sky apart from radio sources. Our approach combines multi-scale wavelet decomposition with conditional flow matching models, trained on $\sim$9$\times$9 deg$^2$ patches at 1-arcminute resolution. The four fields are generated jointly, including their cross couplings, and the model has a tractable likelihood, which to our knowledge has not previously been achieved for this set of components. The power spectra, one-point distributions and Minkowski functionals of the generated maps match a validation set to a few percent. These results show promise for enabling field-level cosmological analyses of mm-wave data.

Using appropriate theoretical arguments, the successful galactic scale dark matter model is generalized to have a universal equation of state of the form $\langle P(χ)\rangle =\langle ρ(χ)\rangle \langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$, where $χ$ is the scale of the system under study and it can describe cosmological or galactic scales and $σ_{DM}$ is the characteristic velocity dispersion of dark matter at the relevant scales. This results to the entropy function behaving universally as $K(χ)=\langle σ_{DM}^2\rangle$. We investigate the cosmological implications of scale-dependent equation of state dark matter, and we conclude that this scale-dependent equation of state dark matter cannot be the sole component of dark matter in the Universe. Also the second aim of this work is to provide a sufficient explanation for the question why $K_0$ changes for each galaxy, where $K_0$ is the value of $K(r)$ at intermediate and large radii from the core of the galaxy. Furthermore, we introduce the concept of having distinct dark matter components which cluster differently, and thus controlling differently the dynamics of the Universe at various scales. Scale-dependent equation of state dark matter can control the galactic scales, while another component can dominate at cosmological scales and thus control the Universe at cosmological scales.

</details>

Orbit sum rules, which also have applications in quantum chaos, are used to describe the anisotropy structures found in the torus ensembles. They relate the length of orbits, which connect a given point on the surface of last scattering with the topological mirror points of the CMB observer, to the anisotropy pattern. Furthermore, the anisotropy of the normalised modulus of the gradient of the CMB temperature field is discussed.

Abstract: Recent results from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) hint at dynamical dark energy, making the accurate modelling of such cosmologies increasingly important for future analyses. As a first, observationally relevant, application of this new method, we match a Chevallier--Polarski--Linder (CPL) cosmology to a constant-$w$ cosmology with the closest $a(τ)$. This allows us to use a single fixed-$w$ simulation to reproduce the simulation predictions of the target CPL cosmology at the field level at redshifts $0\le z\le3$. For most models we consider, we achieve subpercent error in the power spectrum up to scales $k\lesssim1\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ at all redshifts, with density stochasticity below 0.03\% over the same scales. Abstract: We show that clustering in general dynamical dark energy models $w(z)$ can be predicted with constant dark energy $w_0\text{CDM}$ models at the field level via spectral equivalence. Abstract: Cosmological analyses of Type Ia supernovae use a covariance matrix to describe statistical noise and systematic
  uncertainties. To fit a Hubble diagram with an unbinned covariance matrix, the CPU cost scales as O(N^3) for N
  supernovae, which has motivated compression into bins that loses information and reduces self-calibration power. To
  remove the need for lossy compression, we present a method that greatly reduces CPU time for matrix inversion and
  cosmology fitting, as well as matrix storage. For systematic covariances constructed from K per-supernova
  distance-shift vectors, we retain the exact representation C = D + U U^T, with diagonal statistical covariance D,
  and use the Woodbury factorization identity to efficiently invert C, significantly reducing computing time and disk
  storage without losing information. We implement this method in SNANA and validate it on simulated samples for LSST
  (N ~ 5,300, K = 18), Roman+LSST (N ~ 15,000, K = 30), and 10-yr LSST (N ~ 134,400, K = 18). For 10-yr LSST, matrix
  inversion and cosmology fit time is ~10 weeks using a dense (unbinned) matrix with 18 billion elements, and is
  reduced to ~1.5 hr using the factorization method. It also reduces covariance matrix disk storage by a factor of
  1,800 (36 GB to 20 MB). In all validation tests, the best-fit cosmological parameters are nearly identical to the
  unbinned fit, and the dark energy figure of merit (FoM) agrees to better than 0.1% accuracy; the small differences
  are likely due to numerical artifacts in the pipeline. For the LSST and Roman era, this factorization method enables
  large unbinned supernova analyses with significantly reduced computing resources compared to previous analyses using
  a dense matrix, while preserving all information in the covariance model.

</details>

Abstract: Density-field reconstruction sharpens the baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) feature and recovers part of the cosmological information lost from two-point statistics through nonlinear evolution. Exploiting reconstructed clustering beyond BAO measurements requires modelling nonlinear evolution and galaxy formation physics. We extend the hybrid Lagrangian bias expansion to post-reconstruction galaxy clustering in configuration space and determine its range of validity. We apply a single reconstruction displacement to each Eulerian basis field of a second-order hybrid expansion, retaining the same bias parameters before and after reconstruction. We fit the correlation-function multipoles of subhalo abundance-matching mocks in real and redshift space, considering RecIso and RecSym, and use a Fisher analysis to forecast constraints on $σ_8$ and $Ω_{\rm m}$. The model reproduces the pre- and post-reconstruction multipoles for $r>35 h^{-1}\mathrm{Mpc}$ with a single set of Lagrangian bias parameters. Combining PRE and POST reduces both marginalised uncertainties by about one third and increases the figure of merit by a factor of $\sim2.2$, mainly by breaking degeneracies between bias and cosmology. Pre- and post-reconstruction clustering contain complementary cosmological information and can be modelled within the same hybrid Lagrangian bias expansion. The framework is well suited to emulation and future joint full-shape analyses of galaxy surveys.

The EFT model expands the effects of these velocities perturbatively and absorbs their sensitivity to small scales into counterterms. The TNS-based model keeps the one-point velocity dispersion in an unexpanded damping factor and treats it as a single free parameter. We compare both models with power spectra measured from halos in $N$-body simulations and find good agreement on mildly nonlinear scales. These models provide a basis for joint cosmological analyses of galaxy clustering and intrinsic alignments with imaging and spectroscopic galaxy surveys, in which intrinsic alignments are utilized as a cosmological probe.

We test the IGM framework on three reionization simulations (C2-Ray, 21cmFAST, and POLAR) with varying cosmologies, astrophysics, and numerical assumptions. The framework constrains the IGM parameter space, recovers the midpoint and duration of the EoR as well as the minimum mass of halos hosting ionising sources $M_{\mathrm{min}}$, and the redshift evolution of the global ionisation fraction, the evolution of ionised and neutral regions (measured with the radial mean free path method by Georgiev et al. (2026b, submitted)). Moreover, the IGM framework lends itself to joint analysis with other EoR probes, such as high-redshift galaxies and quasars and the Cosmic Microwave Background.

Abstract: Cosmic birefringence, the rotation of the CMB linear polarization plane across the Universe, is a powerful test of parity-violating physics beyond the Standard Model. Static isotropic rotation, however, is limited by an instrument's absolute polarization-angle calibration. We develop two pipelines: a joint LAT+SAT likelihood transferring calibration through their overlap, and the Minami-Komatsu foreground-based method, which separates cosmic birefringence from instrumental miscalibration using Galactic dust polarization without an external angle prior. We derive a general variance budget showing that calibration transfer improves sensitivity only when the combined reference and overlap transfer uncertainties remain below the receiving instrument's calibration uncertainty. While transfer does not lower the absolute-angle floor for isotropic $β$, it anchors the receiving instrument's multipole range. The relative-angle uncertainty is $\sim 0.1^\circ$ for $\ell_{\max}^{\mathrm{SAT}}=300$, improving to $\sim 0.04^\circ$ at $\ell_{\max}^{\mathrm{SAT}}=600$, subject to SAT beam and transfer-function control. Finally, for $β=0.3^\circ$, we show how the LAT's $\ell$-range distinguishes scale-independent dark-energy rotation from scale-dependent axion dark-matter models via $C_\ell^{EB}$.

Abstract: We investigate spherical geodesics in a Newman--Janis-generated rotating regular geometry constructed from the magnetically-charged Bronnikov mass profile. The Kretschmann scalar is exponentially suppressed at the center, and is smooth across the disk and ring within the single-sheeted domain $r\geq0$. We show that all physically admissible spherical photon orbits outside the event horizon are radially unstable in the black-hole regime, whereas stable spherical photon orbits can exist in horizonless configurations. The unstable photon orbits determine the shadow, which for an edge-on observer is smaller and distorted compared with the Kerr shadow. For timelike motion, we identify marginally stable spherical orbits and determine the innermost stable spherical orbit (ISSO). We further examine the null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions, finding violations in the strong-field region but satisfaction of all four conditions asymptotically and throughout the exterior of every black-hole configuration. Together with stable causality and the curvature bound, these results show that the metric satisfies the five geometric criteria C1--C5 proposed by Maeda for physically reasonable nonsingular black holes.

Abstract: We present a generally covariant inflationary model in which curvature history activates a spatial symmetric-tracefree tensor-memory order parameter and generates a scale-dependent quadrupolar departure from primordial statistical isotropy. The curvature source is the four-dimensional Gauss--Bonnet completion of the Kretschmann invariant. A local $(C,χ)$ representation is used only to implement the retarded theory: fixed retarded Cauchy data remove the homogeneous response modes, so the negative-sign localized combination is not an independent phase-space degree of freedom. Conclusion: 高阶WKB+Padé结合独立时域与伪谱验证是必要且可靠的；六阶WKB不足以刻画该参数空间的准正规模。激发因子的大幅偏差主要是规范/坐标原点效应，而非本征的物理放大。

Abstract: We study scalar, electromagnetic, and Dirac perturbations of an asymptotically flat black hole in parity-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. The quasinormal spectrum is computed with very-high-order WKB expansions and Padé resummation, with Cesàro means used to diagnose internal convergence. The dominant modes are independently tested by time-domain evolution with Prony extraction and by Chebyshev pseudospectral collocation; the latter also provides systematic frequency scans in both the metric-scale and physical ADM-mass normalizations. Excitation factors are obtained separately from Green-function residues by complex-contour integration. In the fixed metric-scale normalization, approaching Schwarzschild generally increases both the oscillation frequency and damping rate, whereas stronger deformation shifts the modes toward lower frequencies and weaker damping. In ADM-mass units, the mildly deformed solutions remain close to Schwarzschild, while the most deformed sector becomes enhanced and nonmonotonic because the asymptotic mass itself varies strongly. The independent methods agree very well, however the sixth-order WKB approximation used in [G. Antoniou, T. D. Pappas, P. Kanti, Phys. Rev. D 112, 084013 (2025)] is insufficient in certain regions of the parameter space: the fundamental-mode discrepancy reaches nine percent in the damping rate and the first-overtone discrepancy can be of order unity. The Schwarzschild excitation factors are recovered. In the strongly deformed regime, their magnitudes can differ from the Schwarzschild values by more than two orders of magnitude in the asymptotic tortoise-coordinate convention. We show that this large effect is predominantly due to the deformation-dependent displacement of the potential barrier in the tortoise coordinate: when its maximum is used as the origin, the excitation factors exhibit much more modest deviations.

</details>

On real prograde critical Kerr plunges, $P(r_-)<0$ forces the ingoing time $v$ to diverge logarithmically at the inner horizon while $u_K$ stays finite. Matching $v$ to $-u_K$ at the unique interior zero $P(r_0)=0$ yields a parameter-free onset hierarchy $(u_{\rm H},u_{r_0},u_{\rm C})$ that recovers $9.435M$ as $a\to 0$ and predicts two-stage residual decay and frequency locking at $(6.85M,11.35M,13.35M)$ on an $a/M=0.9$ eccentric plunge.

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric, regular horizonless solutions of quasitopological gravity (QTG), which we call QTG minimons. For an arbitrary characteristic function $h(p)$, we derive a constrained first-order system in the static, asymptotically empty state. We obtain central expansions in $D\ge4$ and analyze four-dimensional solutions in the Hayward-type and Born--Infeld-type models. Both models retain a de Sitter-like core. Along the near-critical branch, the asymptotic mass decreases with the quantum coupling, while the central blueshift can become large even for a small coupling. The numerical results indicate that vacuum polarization is a nonuniform perturbation near the critical limit. Assuming a smooth constant-radius limit in proper distance, we derive a candidate $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^{D-2}$ throat geometry for the limiting configurations, which we call QTG extremons.

Conclusion: 在混合态探测器模型中,相干性比纠缠更具鲁棒性:纠缠可被加速度完全"杀死",而相干性仅渐近衰减。结果凸显了初始混合度作为相对论量子信息中一个相关要素的重要性,并区分了两种量子资源在加速参考系中的不同衰减行为。

Abstract: States prepared in laboratory experiments are generally mixed because of imperfect preparation and residual coupling to the environment, whereas pure initial states represent an idealized limit. Motivated by this distinction, we investigate quantum entanglement and coherence in a pair of Unruh-DeWitt detectors initially prepared in a general two-qubit X state. One detector remains inertial and uncoupled, while the other undergoes uniform acceleration and interacts locally with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We obtain the reduced detector state within a perturbative treatment and evaluate its concurrence, $l_1$ norm of coherence, and relative entropy of coherence. For the initial-state families considered, the resulting expressions exhibit entanglement sudden death at finite acceleration, with larger initial entanglement associated with a higher threshold. Both coherence measures remain nonzero beyond this threshold and approach zero in the formal infinite-acceleration limit of these expressions. We also examine the dependence of these resources on the detector energy gap and interaction duration. The results distinguish the persistence of coherence from that of entanglement in the mixed-state detector model and identify initial mixedness as a relevant ingredient in relativistic quantum information.

</details>

Result: 离散理论等价于纯Regge引力耦合scalaron与axion两个标量场；导出的离散方程在演化时间步长的一阶精度上重现连续闭合解；给出了正则三角剖分下的精确动能与曲率系数。

Abstract: We formulate $f(R)$ gravity with spacetime torsion in Regge calculus, but without an \emph{ad hoc} geometric definition of torsion. Working in D'Adda's formulation of simplicial gravity with coordinates, we show that the axial sector of the torsion is carried by a massless Kalb--Ramond two-form, which in four dimensions is dual to a single scalar on the dual vertices. The discretized theory is therefore pure Regge gravity coupled to two scalar fields, the scalaron and the axion, without a geometric definition of torsion on the Regge lattice. We derive the lattice action and the complete set of discrete equations of motion, and we verify the continuum limit. For a homogeneous and isotropic lattice we obtain the discrete Friedmann--Raychaudhuri system, compute the exact kinetic and curvature coefficients for a regular triangulation of $\mathbb S^3$, and show that the discrete equations reproduce the continuum closed solution at first order in the evolution time step. The results therefore present a very special case in which the torsional effects are completely described by scalar fields on the Regge lattice and there is no need of a full discrete differential calculus.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate birefringent imaging, interferometric visibility, neutral periodic geodesics, and thin-disk radiation around a magnetically charged black hole in Einstein-nonlinearelectrodynamics, using the Euler-Heisenberg-type model of de Paula et al. [14]. The nonlinear coupling is treated as a free model parameter. Two effective photon metrics give rise to distinct unstable light rings, capture thresholds, and images. Transforming their image-order intensities into signed Fourier visibilities reveals separate critical-curve oscillations and a narrow-ring modulation scale in the unresolved sum. This two-mode correspondence does not require photon motion between two potential barriers. Rational orbital frequencies are then used to classify neutral bound trajectories and provide conservative gravitationalwave snapshots relevant to extreme-mass-ratio inspirals. A stationary thin-disk calculation supplies flux, temperature, emitted efficiency, and polarization-resolved thermal spectra. Across the sampled parameters, magnetic charge dominates the changes, while matched Maxwell comparisons resolve smaller nonlinear corrections. The static, optically thin emitters and the orbiting, optically thick thermal disk are distinct source configurations. Polarization comparisons use the same material source within each configuration; their brightness and spectral results are conditional on that configuration.

</details>


### [74] [Viscous damping of axisymmetric oscillation modes of uniformly rotating relativistic stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29617)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the effects of bulk and shear viscosity on the damping of axisymmetric oscillations of uniformly rotating relativistic stars, using the first-order viscous hydrodynamics formulation of Bemfica, Disconzi, Noronha and Kovtun (BDNK). The equations of motion are implemented in a two-dimensional numerical code under the Cowling approximation, assuming the fluid evolves on the fixed spacetime of a rotating star. We build two sequences of polytropic stellar models and investigate how damping depends on rotation rate (from the non-rotating case to the mass-shedding limit) and compactness. Our study focuses on the quasi-radial $l=0$ $F$ mode and the $l=1,2,3$ fundamental modes, covering a dataset of 288 perturbed stellar models. For all modes and chosen viscosities, the BDNK runs show that oscillations decay within a few milliseconds, much faster than in the perfect fluid case. The $F$ mode damping is dominated by bulk viscosity, consistent with its global compression of the star, while the $l=2$ and $l=3$ modes are mainly damped by shear viscosity. The $l=1$ mode is largely insensitive to both viscosity coefficients, and its decay rate is dominated by numerical dissipation. Rotation renders the $F$ mode sensitive to shear viscosity. Since the inherent numerical damping does not affect the slopes of the decay rates, our viscous sensitivity estimates faithfully identify the dominant damping mechanism for each mode, shear or bulk viscosity, and reveal robust trends with rotation rate and compactness.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Our approach employs a satellite constellation comprising one satellite in low Earth orbit (LEO) and one satellite in distant retrograde orbit (DRO). This configuration is designed to enhance the sensitivity to the Earth's rotation-induced frame-dragging signal while providing a stable and gravitationally isolated frequency reference. We derive a complete relativistic formulation for the frequency comparison between LEO and DRO, incorporating the post-Newtonian metric, higher-order Earth multipoles, lunar and solar tidal potentials, and the gravitomagnetic vector potential. Abstract: Starting from any compact sub-extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region with positive cosmological constant, we construct a family of static axisymmetric vacuum solutions with two additional black-hole horizons, each of prescribed area. All horizons and the symmetry axis are regular, and the metric and lapse are real analytic up to the boundary. As the mass parameter tends to zero, the metric and lapse converge smoothly to the reference solution away from two antipodal points on its sphere of maximal lapse. Rescaling near either added horizon yields the Schwarzschild spatial metric in the limit. For small prescribed mass, the lapse attains its maximum precisely on one equatorial circle. Its Hessian is negative definite in the transverse plane, with the smaller eigenvalue magnitude asymptotic to a positive constant times the added mass. Nearby regular level sets are tori. The proof combines gluing at separated scales with uniform elliptic estimates. An integral condition removes infinitesimal translations of each Schwarzschild model, while a localized tensor source restores solvability of the linearized equation. Matching the local Schwarzschild corrections to a global Green tensor identifies the leading source coefficients. Reflection makes these coefficients equal, and varying the common radial position of the centers makes them vanish. A radial Green function and the heat equation on the sphere determine the angular variation of the lapse and the geometry of its maximum set.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and the stability of circular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a Kiselev anisotropic fluid, characterised by the equation-of-state parameter $ω$ and the normalisation parameter $N$. We consider six representative values, $ω=-1,-2/3,-1/3,0,1/3$ and $1$, over the admissible range of $N$ determined by the horizon structure, including the corresponding extremal limits. Using the effective potentials for null and timelike geodesics, we determine the photon sphere, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the outermost stable circular orbit (OSCO), and examine their stability through coordinate-time Lyapunov exponents. The photon sphere is unstable for all values of $ω$ and $N$ considered. Its instability weakens with increasing $N$ for $ω\leq0$ and vanishes at extremality for $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, whereas it varies non-monotonically with $N$ for $ω=1/3$ and strengthens for $ω=1$. For $ω=-1$ and $-2/3$, stable circular orbits are confined between the ISCO and the OSCO, which merge at a critical value of $N$ beyond which no stable circular orbits exist. In contrast, for $ω\geq-1/3$ stable circular orbits exist at all radii beyond the ISCO, whose location varies with $N$. We also derive a closed-form expression for the squared coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent $(λ_t^T)^2$, valid for arbitrary $ω$, whose sign changes exactly at the ISCO and OSCO, so that the Lyapunov criterion reproduces the boundaries of stable circular motion. The Schwarzschild--de Sitter, mass-shifted Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström geometries are recovered as special cases, and the results may have implications for black-hole shadows, thin accretion discs and quasi-periodic oscillations.

</details>

Motivation: LISA观测的极端质量比旋进（EMRI）可用于探测大质量黑洞周围环境，包括预期在其周围形成的暗物质（DM）过密区；此前关于动力学摩擦与吸积的结果仅限于Schwarzschild背景，需推广到旋转黑洞并检验常用近似公式的可靠性。

Conclusion: 建模DM对EMRI的影响时必须纳入环境的完整相空间结构，而非仅依赖局部密度的近似处方。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) observed by LISA will probe the environments of massive black holes (BHs), including the dark matter (DM) overdensities expected to form around them. We compute the relativistic dynamical friction and accretion experienced by a non-spinning compact object on circular equatorial orbits around a Kerr BH embedded in a collisionless DM distribution, extending previous results in Schwarzschild. The DM is modeled as a spike grown adiabatically from an initial Hernquist profile, and the momentum transfer is obtained by integrating relativistic scattering and capture cross sections over the full, generically anisotropic phase space seen by the secondary. Far from the primary the friction tracks the local DM density, which increases with spin. Near the innermost stable circular orbit of rapidly spinning BHs $(a\gtrsim 0.37)$, however, frame dragging forces bound DM particles to co-rotate, so that the secondary is overtaken by the surrounding medium: the friction weakens and eventually reverses sign where the DM density peaks. We show that a similar reversal can be mimicked around a non-rotating BH if the initial halo carries large net angular momentum, although this requires anisotropies beyond those found in cosmological simulations. We also show that commonly used Chandrasekhar-like prescriptions, which depend only on the local density, can overestimate the friction force. Our results demonstrate that the full phase-space structure of the environment must be included when modeling DM effects on EMRIs.

Abstract: Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Since binaries formed in isolation are expected to circularize long before merger, measurable eccentricity suggests dynamical formation in dense stellar environments. However, using numerical-relativity simulations, we show that eccentricity can be introduced in the late inspiral of an initially quasicircular binary, due to the $f$-mode resonance of one of the stars. We simulate the merger of two highly spinning neutron stars, one star spinning prograde and the other retrograde. Persistent tidal resonance with the $f$ mode of the retrograde-spinning star is observed, accompanied by a gradual change of its spin due to angular momentum transfer from the orbit. This spin change can be well explained by a simple resonance locking model that accounts for the spin-dependent shift of the $f$-mode frequency. These findings reveal an origin of eccentricity in binary neutron stars that is independent of their formation history and provide important information for modeling binary neutron star dynamics with rapidly spinning stars.

Abstract: Astrometric measurements have emerged as a viable probe of gravitational waves (GWs), complementing pulsar timing searches by providing overlapping and higher GW frequency coverage. At its core, the search for GWs using astrometry is very similar to the search with pulsar timing data: GWs induce a change in pulse arrival times along the line of sight to a pulsar and a transverse angular deflection in the apparent position of a star. In analogy to pulsar timing, we develop a correlation statistic between pairs of stellar deflections for astrometry to search for a stochastic GW background. With a common formalism for both astrometry and pulsar timing, we also develop the cross-correlation statistic between stellar deflections and pulsar timing residuals. We estimate the signal-to-noise ratios from the correlation statistics and provide scaling laws in the weak- and intermediate-signal regimes.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延展体（自旋、潮汐形变）在弯曲时空中的保守运动方程与守恒量结构缺乏统一表述；黑洞双星是否存在区别于一般致密天体的隐藏对称性尚不清楚，需要系统研究多极阶展开下的可积性。

Method: 以运动学质量 $\mu$ 与协变标量势 $U$ 构造哈密顿表述 $H=-\tfrac12\mu^2+\mu U$，将任意阶自旋诱导与绝热潮汐多极矩统一纳入 $U$；证明特定质量概念与自旋范数的严格守恒；在Kerr背景中逐阶构造Carter常数与Rüdiger常数的多极推广至十六极阶（自旋四次），并用解析判据（数值实现）检验五阶自旋的可延拓性。

Result: Carter与Rüdiger常数可推广至十六极阶，当且仅当多极矩取Kerr黑洞值；结合能量与角动量，自旋测试黑洞在Kerr中的动力学在该阶为微扰可积。在五阶自旋处，解析判据表明两常数对任何保守多极矩均无延拓。

Conclusion: 保守测试体极限下，黑洞双星具有其他致密天体不具备的动力学隐藏对称性（额外守恒量）；该对称性可延续至很高的多极阶，但最终必然破缺。

Abstract: We show that the conservative equations of motion of an extended test body, with force and torque sourced by spin-induced and adiabatic tidal multipoles of arbitrary order, are the flow of the Hamiltonian $H=-\tfrac12μ^2+μU$, where $μ$ is the body's kinematic mass and all multipoles enter through a covariant scalar potential $U$. For such bodies, a certain notion of mass and a spin norm are shown to be exactly conserved, at all multipolar orders and in any background. We then apply our framework to the Kerr spacetime, and construct extensions of the Carter and Rüdiger constants through hexadecapolar order (quartic in spin). They are found to exist if, and only if, the body's multipoles are those of a Kerr black hole. Together with the energy and angular momentum, they make the dynamics of a spinning test black hole in Kerr perturbatively integrable through that order. Lastly, at the next, quintic-in-spin order, an analytic criterion implemented numerically reveals that neither constant admits a continuation, for any conservative multipoles. In the conservative, test-body limit, black-hole binaries thus carry a dynamical hidden symmetry shared by no other compact objects, which persists to high multipolar order but ultimately breaks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hohenberg–Kohn定理确立了基态下势与粒子密度的一一对应，是密度泛函理论（DFT）的基石。但其向激发态的推广是否成立尚无定论；作者希望检验激发态情形下势—密度映射的单射性是否失效，从而澄清HK定理在激发态的可推广边界。

Method: 在一维单位环面上研究n个非相互作用费米子，取重标度Lamé势 v_*(x)=2mℓ² sn²(ℓx;m)（与Jacobi椭圆函数/有限带势相关），显式分析其单粒子谱与第一激发态波函数，计算第一激发态密度；通过势的平移构造单参数族；对奇数n在谱非共振假设下作类似分析；n=2时借助平面elastica分类进行讨论。

Result: 对每个n=2l，存在常数m∈(0,1)与ℓ>0，使得该系统具有简单第一激发能，且第一激发态粒子密度在环面上为常数；平移势得到非平凡单参数族势均给出同一常数第一激发态密度，故势—密度映射非单射。在谱非共振假设下，奇数n≥3情形在简单激发能处得到同样的非唯一性。

Conclusion: Hohenberg–Kohn定理对激发态一般不成立：激发态密度不能唯一确定势，基态DFT的一一对应不可直接推广到激发态；作者还指出与n=2时平面elastica分类问题的联系，并提出若干待研究问题。

Abstract: For an even number of non-interacting fermions on the one-dimensional unit torus, a semi-explicit counterexample to the Hohenberg--Kohn theorem for excited states is exhibited. Indeed, for each $n = 2l$, it is shown that there exist constants $m \in (0, 1)$ and $\ell > 0$ such that an $n$-body system with the rescaled Lamé potential $v_*(x) = 2m \ell^2 \operatorname{sn}^2(\ell x; m)$ has a simple first excited energy and a first excited-state particle density which is constant on the torus. By translating this potential, we exhibit a non-trivial one-parameter family of potentials, all of which have a constant first excited-state density. As such, the potential-to-density map is not one-to-one in this setting. Under a spectral nonresonance assumption, we obtain the same nonuniqueness at a simple excited energy for odd $n \geq 3$. We also explore connections to the classification problem of elastica in the plane when $n = 2$, and outline some related questions of interest.

Method: 采用多重尺度（多尺度）渐近展开：将波包按未扰动的紧束缚色散关系谱局部化于某点 $(K,E)\in\mathbb{R}^d\times\mathbb{R}$，以渐近参数组织包络的慢变尺度；在含简并的谱点处，将态投影到相关的退化子空间上，构造有效哈密顿量，并显式刻画退化能带与其余能带之间的耦合（带间耦合项）。

Conclusion: 多重尺度展开可系统地为慢变紧束缚薛定谔方程提供高阶、长时间有效的宏观包络方程；退化谱点不再构成障碍，通过退化子空间上的有效哈密顿量可正确纳入带间耦合，理论具有一般性并有数值支持。

Abstract: By a multiple-scale asymptotic expansion, we derive effective macroscopic models for a large class of tight-binding Schrödinger equations with slow spatial variations. For wave packets that are spectrally localized to some point $(K;E) \in \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$ in the unperturbed tight-binding dispersion relation, our effective models produce long-time approximations of the tight-binding dynamics, accurate to any prescribed order in the asymptotic parameter. Our framework allows the point $(K;E)$ to be degenerate, meaning that multiple (but not necessarily all) energy bands of the tight-binding Hamiltonian can attain the value $E$ at the frequency $K$. We construct an effective Hamiltonian on the relevant degenerate subspace, which quantifies the coupling between the degenerate and other bands. Several applications and numerical simulations illustrate the theory.

</details>

Conclusion: 仿射Temperley-Lieb模具有良定的辫子张量与ribbon结构，其（半单）子范畴的单模与$U_q(sl_2)$最高权模对存在对应，为后续严格证明该等价提供了依据。

Abstract: We prove that the class of all affine Temperley-Lieb modules, equipped with the recently introduced "diagrammatic" tensor product, forms a quasitensor (i.e. monoidal braided) category. When the Temperley-Lieb parameter $q$ is not a root of unity, we consider the infinite subcategory generated by the simple quotients of reducible standard modules, and we argue that it is a semi-simple ribbon category. In the $p$ sector the coordinate functions carry an exact spin-one action, and applying $D$ componentwise maps the confocal ellipsoid to a sphere. Elliptic fusion gives explicit $R$-matrices in the angular generators on both factors. Their Yang--Baxter relations yield commuting charges and exchange Hamiltonians for homogeneous and alternating chains, as well as mixed-pair interactions that can act between angular degrees of freedom on the same center. Exchange changes angular projections and correlations while preserving the two local Casimirs of each constituent, which fix its angular-momentum-squared and distortion-plus-rotation kinetic-energy invariants. The $p$--$p$ model recovers Fateev's elliptic spin-one interaction and reduces to the Takhtajan--Babujian Hamiltonian in the rational isotropic limit.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳体理论中应变需满足相容性条件，而应力需满足平衡条件，但二者缺乏一个统一的、具有良好上同调性质的算子复形框架，难以统一刻画可解性、势函数表示与分解结构。

Result: 得到的壳体复形是无穷小刚体运动层的精细消解，其上同调群由相应层上同调决定；复形揭示了平衡应力场的Airy势表示；框架同时给出了运动学与动力学变量的自然边界条件；并在带边界壳体上建立了应变场与应力场的Hodge–Morrey–Friedrichs分解。

Conclusion: 该工作为Kirchhoff–Love壳体理论提供了完整的弹性复形结构，把相容性、平衡性、势表示、边界条件与分解统一在同一框架内，可为壳体的混合有限元与稳定离散格式设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: We develop an elasticity complex for Kirchhoff--Love shell theory, based on the linearized Gauss--Codazzi equations as compatibility conditions for shell strains. The resulting shell complex forms a fine resolution of the sheaf of infinitesimal rigid motions, so its cohomology groups are determined by the corresponding sheaf cohomology. Remarkably, the complex also reveals an Airy potential representation of equilibrium stress fields. The framework further identifies natural boundary conditions for both kinematic and dynamic variables. Finally, we establish a Hodge--Morrey--Friedrichs decomposition for strain and stress fields on shells with boundary.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 矩阵Friedrichs模型为二阶Feshbach共振提供了显式可解范例；共振阶数的提升对应转移函数算子根结构的退化/重根现象，这为一般算子框架下高阶共振的识别提供了判据。

Abstract: We consider an extended version of the matrix Friedrichs model that may produce Feshbach resonances of the second order. In this model, under enlarging of the coupling constant, a pair of eigenvalues of the unperturbed Hamiltonian embedded into the continuous spectrum first transforms in resonances of the first order. At some moment later on, these two resonances merge into a single resonance but of the second order, and we fix conditions for this event. Being based on the operator interpretation of resonances, we found that, in the case under consideration, the operator roots of the transfer function have a very particular structure.

</details>
